specs/system-deep-loop/loop-architecture/004-workflow-logic-hardening/001-deterministic-stop-decision/plan.md:63:`step_graph_convergence` runs `convergence.cjs` once with `--stop-decision` and the input paths. The script reads the graph as today, then reads the config for `maxIterations`, `minIterations`, `convergenceThreshold`, `stopPolicy`, `stuckThreshold` and `convergenceMode`, reads `state.jsonl` for the evidence iterations and the latest `claim_adjudication` event and reads the registry for questions, dimensions and search coverage. It evaluates in this fixed order: max-iteration cap, stuck threshold, `convergenceMode off` and `stopPolicy max-iterations`, STOP candidates (`all_questions_answered` or `all_dimensions_clean` and the composite vote), minimum-iteration floor, research quality guards or review legal-stop gates (including the claim-adjudication veto), then the graph rule. The payload gains `stop_decision` and the workflow branches on it.

          - graph_digest: "sha256 (64 lowercase hex) of graph_signals_json"

      step_check_convergence:
        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the review config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out thought iterations and every iteration whose status is error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap is evaluated before every veto, the claim-adjudication veto included; failed legal-stop gates are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every dimension is clean (all_dimensions_clean) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged). The vote cutoffs follow the configured convergenceThreshold.
          4a. if iteration_count < min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached). min_iterations is config.minIterations, else antiConvergence.minIterations, capped at max_iterations; like research, the floor runs on a candidate before the legal-stop gates.
          5. the nine legal-stop gates run on a candidate: convergenceGate, dimensionCoverageGate, p0ResolutionGate, evidenceDensityGate, hotspotSaturationGate, claimAdjudicationGate, fixCompletenessReplayGate, candidateCoverageGate, graphlessFallbackGate. Any failed gate gives BLOCKED (blockedStop).
          6. the graph decision settles the candidate: STOP_ALLOWED keeps STOP, STOP_BLOCKED gives BLOCKED, CONTINUE or a missing graph decision gives CONTINUE.
          Bind the outputs from stop_decision, where G is stop_decision.gates and I is stop_decision.inputs.review:
            decision = stop_decision.decision
            stopReason = stop_decision.stopReason
            reason = stop_decision.stopReason when it is not null, else stop_decision.reasonDetail
            reason_detail = stop_decision.reasonDetail
            weighted_stop_score = stop_decision.weightedStopScore
            stop_blocked = (stop_decision.decision == "BLOCKED")
            blocked_by_json = stop_decision.blockedBy
            blocked_by_csv = the members of stop_decision.blockedBy joined with commas
            convergence_gate_pass = G.convergenceGate.pass
            convergence_gate_score = G.convergenceGate.score
            dimension_coverage_gate_pass = G.dimensionCoverageGate.pass
            dimension_coverage_gate_missing_json = G.dimensionCoverageGate.missing
            dimension_coverage_gate_covered_json = the configured review dimensions that are not in G.dimensionCoverageGate.missing

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/convergence/convergence.md:107:After the review floor, an expansive run that still holds an open or active node turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`, whichever candidate it was, `all_dimensions_clean` or a composite convergence vote. The veto never creates a stop of its own and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the `minIterations` floor. Under stuck recovery the handler closes the active node as `exhausted` with reason `stuck`, so a node the run cannot fund stops holding the veto open.

// Full-lineage lifetime is non-disableable in autonomous mode: 4 hours is the hard
// ceiling and a positive override may only narrow it, never widen past it.
const LINEAGE_LIFETIME_HARD_MAX_HOURS = 4;

function assertLineageTimeoutHoursOverrideWithinCeiling(ceilingHoursOverride) {
  if (Number.isFinite(ceilingHoursOverride) && ceilingHoursOverride > LINEAGE_LIFETIME_HARD_MAX_HOURS) {
    throw inputError(
      `lineageTimeoutHours override (${ceilingHoursOverride}) exceeds the hard maximum of `
        + `${LINEAGE_LIFETIME_HARD_MAX_HOURS} hours`,
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Compute a generous timeout for a full lineage loop run.
 * A single iteration is timeoutSeconds; a full loop is up to iterations * timeoutSeconds.
 * We double it for safety and cap at 4 hours. A caller-provided ceiling override may only
 * narrow this hard ceiling, never widen it — full-lineage lifetime is non-disableable.
 */
function computeLineageTimeoutMs(lineage, ceilingHoursOverride) {
  assertLineageTimeoutHoursOverrideWithinCeiling(ceilingHoursOverride);
  const iters = lineage.iterations || 12;
  const perIterSecs = lineage.timeoutSeconds || 900;
  const ceilingMs = Number.isFinite(ceilingHoursOverride) && ceilingHoursOverride > 0

commit 9e0b02c49a6178776643ea85c9d1c9aada99d3f4
Author: Michel Kerkmeester <redacted>
Date:   Wed Nov 18 01:18:47 2026 +0100

    fix(system-deep-loop): repair expansive review frontier defects from the pilot
    
    A live expansive review pilot over a 400-commit range found four defects on
    the frontier path. The root shared the per-node children cap, so a review seed
    admitted six depth-1 sub-targets although the workflow promises three to eight;
    the root cap is now the top of each loop's documented seed range (eight for
    review, six for research) and never below the per-node cap, which stays as it
    was. A commit-range target typed as a file list made the range string the only
    allowed path, so every depth-2 proposal pruned as irrelevant; relevance now
    intersects a range target with the files scope discovery recorded for it.
    Merged frontier nodes kept lineage-local ids, so three lineages each reported
    F-2-002; merged ids and parent links are now lineage-qualified, with the local
    id kept beside them for the partition guard. The review docs now name the
    frontier_partition key the runner records in orchestration-summary.json.
    
    Every change sits on the expansive path; both depth-mode golden suites pass.
    
    Commit-Id: 0014839

+// A git revision range (base..tip or base...tip) is not a path. The dots must sit

+function isCommitRange(value) {

+// A commit range names no paths itself; the files it touches are its scope, and

+function scopeInsideTouchedFiles(scope, touchedFiles) {

# DR-068 -- Expansive depth flag and default byte identity

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DR-068`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates the `--depth-mode` flag and its default. The objective is to confirm that a research run without the flag, or with `--depth-mode=default`, produces the same registry, dashboard, Next Focus, prompt pack and stop config as the loop produced before the expansive mode existed.

### Why This Matters

The expansive mode must stay opt-in. An operator who never passes the flag should see no change in what the loop writes or decides. That promise lets the mode ship without a migration.

---

# DR-072 -- Frontier open veto holds a soft stop

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DR-072`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates the `frontierOpen` veto in the stop decision. The objective is to confirm that a soft stop waits while frontier nodes stay open, and that every hard rule keeps its precedence.

### Why This Matters

A research run that answers its init questions narrowly looks converged. The veto keeps the run going while the frontier still holds open work. It must never outrank a hard limit, or a run could loop past its budget.

---
title: "DR-072 -- Frontier open veto holds a soft stop"
description: "This scenario validates that under the expansive mode an open frontier node turns a composite convergence stop into CONTINUE, and that the cap, the floor, stuck recovery, convergence off and the max-iterations policy still decide first, for `DR-072`."
version: 1.16.0.0
---

# DR-072 -- Frontier open veto holds a soft stop

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DR-072`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates the `frontierOpen` veto in the stop decision. The objective is to confirm that a soft stop waits while frontier nodes stay open, and that every hard rule keeps its precedence.

### Why This Matters

A research run that answers its init questions narrowly looks converged. The veto keeps the run going while the frontier still holds open work. It must never outrank a hard limit, or a run could loop past its budget.

---

## 2. SCENARIO CONTRACT

Operators run the exact commands below and confirm the veto precedence table and the unchanged input echo.

- Objective: Confirm that an open node vetoes a composite convergence stop only under the expansive mode, and that the cap, the floor, stuck recovery, convergence off and the max-iterations policy keep their own decisions.
- Real user request: Explain why the run did not stop when it looked converged, and show that the hard limits still stop it.
- Prompt: `Check that under expansive depth mode a composite convergence stop continues while frontier nodes are open, and that the cap, the floor, stuck recovery, convergence off and the max-iterations policy still decide first. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.`
- Expected execution process: Run the veto table by file stem, run the input echo case, then read the veto branch and the research facts in the stop-decision script.
- Expected signals: The veto table passes all eight rows. The row for open nodes returns `CONTINUE` with `frontierOpen`. The row for an empty frontier returns `STOP` with `composite_converged`. The cap, the floor, stuck recovery, convergence off and max-iterations rows keep their own decisions. The echo case passes unedited.
- Desired user-visible outcome: The operator can say a soft stop waits for open frontier nodes, and the hard limits still stop the run on time.
- Pass/fail: PASS if the table and the echo case pass and the script shows the veto after the floor; FAIL if an open node lets a stop through, or a hard limit loses to the veto.

---

## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### Prerequisites

- Working directory is the repository root.
- The `runtime/` vitest suites are installed under `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime`.

### Prompt

- Prompt: `Check that under expansive depth mode a composite convergence stop continues while frontier nodes are open, and that the cap, the floor, stuck recovery, convergence off and the max-iterations policy still decide first. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.`

### Commands

1. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run stop-decision.vitest -t "frontierOpen veto" --reporter=verbose`
2. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run stop-decision.vitest -t "echoes the input unchanged" --reporter=verbose`
3. `rg -n "frontierOpen" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs`

### Expected

Command 1 prints `Tests  28 passed | 64 skipped (92)`, because the filter also matches the review veto cases. Those cases include the eight rows of the research veto table, each named `expansive composite candidate: <row label>`, and every row passes. Command 2 prints `Tests  1 passed | 91 skipped (92)`, and the case `echoes the input unchanged` passes without edits. Command 3 prints the veto branch that returns `CONTINUE` with `frontierOpen`, and the research facts line that counts open and active nodes.

### Evidence

- The verbose case lines and summary line from commands 1 and 2, with each exit status.
- The `rg` lines from command 3, read from the current source.

### Pass / Fail

- **Pass**: Commands 1 and 2 exit 0 with the expected counts, and command 3 shows the veto after the floor check.
- **Fail**: An open node lets a stop through, a hard limit loses to the veto, or the echo case fails.

### Failure Triage

- An open node lets a stop through: check that the veto reads the expansive flag and the `frontierOpen` count from the research facts.
- A hard limit loses to the veto: check the order of the veto and the cap, floor and stuck checks in the decision.
- The echo case fails: check that the stop-decision config echo adds `depthMode` only for an expansive run.

---
- Unknown fields are warnings, not errors.
- Malformed lines, including missing `:`, emit a parse error naming the offending line. Known fields parsed before the error may still be used, and unresolved fields continue to Tier 2 or Tier 3.
- Empty strings count as unresolved for required fields.
- Compact legacy answer strings are only for the consolidated `:confirm` prompt. They are not a `:auto` marker format.

### Default Resolution Table

| Field | Required | Resolves Via | Default | Tier-2 Candidate |
|-------|----------|--------------|---------|------------------|
| `review_target` | Y | `$ARGUMENTS` first positional target, or marker `review_target` | none | N |
| `review_target_type` | Y | marker `review_target_type`, or auto-detect from `review_target` | inferred only | Y, when target is present but ambiguous |
| `review_dimensions` | Y | flag `--dims` if supported by caller, marker `review_dimensions`, or default | `"all"` | N |
| `review_focus` | N | flag `--review-focus="TEXT"`, marker `review_focus`, or default. Control characters and whitespace runs collapse to one space, an empty result is absent, and more than 1000 characters fails setup | empty (dimensions only) | N |
| `spec_folder` | Y | flag `--spec-folder`, marker `spec_folder`, `scope-extract` → a target/scope path that resolves to an existing spec folder (auto-bound, per `auto_mode_contract` §1 source 3), or target path when target is a spec folder | none | Y, when target is not a spec folder |
| `execution_mode` | Y | attached suffix `:auto` or marker `execution_mode` | `AUTONOMOUS` under `:auto` | N |
| `lineage_mode` | Y | flag `--restart`, flag `--lineage-mode=auto|resume|restart`, marker `lineage_mode`, or default | `auto` | N |
| `extend` | N | flag `--extend=N` (positive integer); sets `lineage_mode=completed-continue` on a completed run; refused with fan-out | none | N |
| `extend_minutes` | N | flag `--extend-minutes=M` (positive integer), valid only with `--extend` | the prior `maxDurationMinutes` | N |
| `maxIterations` | Y | flag `--max-iterations`, marker `maxIterations`, or default | `7` | N |
| `convergenceThreshold` | Y | flag `--convergence`, marker `convergenceThreshold`, or default | `0.10` | N |
| `convergence_mode` | Y | flag `--convergence-mode=default|off|sliding-window|divergent`, marker `convergence_mode`, or default | `default` | N |
| `depth_mode` | Y | flag `--depth-mode=default|expansive`, marker `depth_mode`, or default `default` | `default` | N |
| `stop_policy` | Y | flag `--stop-policy=convergence|max-iterations`, marker `stop_policy`, or default | `convergence` | N |
| `executor` | N | flag `--executor`, marker `executor`, config file, or default | `native` | N |
| `executor_model` | N | flag `--model`, marker `executor_model`, or executor-specific validation | none | N |
| `executor_config_dir` | N | flag `--config-dir`, marker `executor_config_dir`, or executor-specific default | none | N |
| `executor_reasoning` | N | flag `--reasoning-effort`, marker `executor_reasoning`, or executor default | none | N |
| `executor_service_tier` | N | flag `--service-tier`, marker `executor_service_tier`, or executor default | none | N |
| `executor_timeout` | N | flag `--executor-timeout`, marker `executor_timeout`, or default | `900` | N |
| `resource_map_emit` | N | flag `--no-resource-map`, marker `resource_map_emit`, or default | `true` | N |
| `config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir` | N | internal flag `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir=PATH` or marker `config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir` | none | N |
| `fanout_executors` | N | repeatable `--executor=<type>` flags or `--executors=<json>`; each group accepts `--model`, `--config-dir`, `--reasoning-effort`, `--service-tier`, `--executor-timeout`, `--iters`, `--label`, `--count`; one expanded lineage selects `config.executor`, two or more select `config.fanout` | none (single-executor when absent) | N |
| `fanout_concurrency` | N | flag `--concurrency=N` | `2` | N |

**Fan-out default policy:** 0–1 `--executor` flags and no `--executors`, or an `--executors` matrix that expands to exactly one lineage → `config.executor` (single-executor, default, unchanged). 2+ `--executor` flags, an `--executors` matrix that expands to two or more lineages, or any `--count > 1` → `config.fanout`. Review fan-out uses strongest-restriction: any lineage active P0 → merged FAIL. Native and CLI fan-out lineages run under `fanout-run.cjs` in the capped pool so every lineage shares retries, artifact checks, and stop-policy validation. The native adapter calls the `deep/review` command with the internal `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir` override; it must not dispatch the LEAF `@deep-review` agent or a pasted phase-running prompt as a full-loop sub-agent. When fan-out includes `cli-opencode`, that lineage is an explicit parallel-detached OpenCode session with its own lineage directory and session id; this is the documented exception to the cli-opencode self-invocation guard. Single-executor `cli-opencode` remains cross-runtime only.

**Lifecycle policy:** `lineage_mode=auto` preserves the existing classifier behavior, `lineage_mode=resume` continues a valid packet, and `lineage_mode=restart` archives the resolved `{artifact_dir}` before any config write, phase init, or fan-out spawn. In `:auto`, an explicit `--restart` or `--lineage-mode=restart` flag is operator authorization for this archive move, so do not ask for a second confirmation. Preserve rollback by moving the timestamped archive back to `review/` if needed. A restart request must never silently downgrade to resume; if the archive step cannot run, halt with `STATUS=FAIL`.

**Stop policy:** `stop_policy=convergence` preserves normal deep-review behavior: legal convergence may stop before `maxIterations`. `stop_policy=max-iterations` makes convergence signals telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling; before `maxIterations`, broaden the next angle instead of synthesizing. Terminal manual pause, user stop, unrecoverable error, and `maxIterationsReached` still stop.

93-
94:**Fan-out default policy:** 0–1 `--executor` flags and no `--executors`, or an `--executors` matrix that expands to exactly one lineage → `config.executor` (single-executor, default, unchanged). 2+ `--executor` flags, an `--executors` matrix that expands to two or more lineages, or any `--count > 1` → `config.fanout`. Review fan-out uses strongest-restriction: any lineage active P0 → merged FAIL. Native and CLI fan-out lineages run under `fanout-run.cjs` in the capped pool so every lineage shares retries, artifact checks, and stop-policy validation. The native adapter calls the `deep/review` command with the internal `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir` override; it must not dispatch the LEAF `@deep-review` agent or a pasted phase-running prompt as a full-loop sub-agent. When fan-out includes `cli-opencode`, that lineage is an explicit parallel-detached OpenCode session with its own lineage directory and session id; this is the documented exception to the cli-opencode self-invocation guard. Single-executor `cli-opencode` remains cross-runtime only.
95-
96-**Lifecycle policy:** `lineage_mode=auto` preserves the existing classifier behavior, `lineage_mode=resume` continues a valid packet, and `lineage_mode=restart` archives the resolved `{artifact_dir}` before any config write, phase init, or fan-out spawn. In `:auto`, an explicit `--restart` or `--lineage-mode=restart` flag is operator authorization for this archive move, so do not ask for a second confirmation. Preserve rollback by moving the timestamped archive back to `review/` if needed. A restart request must never silently downgrade to resume; if the archive step cannot run, halt with `STATUS=FAIL`.
97-
98-**Stop policy:** `stop_policy=convergence` preserves normal deep-review behavior: legal convergence may stop before `maxIterations`. `stop_policy=max-iterations` makes convergence signals telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling; before `maxIterations`, broaden the next angle instead of synthesizing. Terminal manual pause, user stop, unrecoverable error, and `maxIterationsReached` still stop.

      'max_token_budget_per_lineage',
    ], 'maxCostUnitsPerLineage') ?? DEFAULT_MAX_COST_UNITS_PER_LINEAGE,
    cost_units_per_iteration: readPositiveRawConfigNumber(rawConfig, [
      'costUnitsPerIteration',

      'estimatedTokensPerIteration',
      'estimated_tokens_per_iteration',
    ], 'costUnitsPerIteration', 1),

function computeLineageBudgetUpperBound(lineage, guardsInput = {}, maxRetries = 0) {
  const guards = normalizeLineageBudgetGuards(guardsInput);
  const rawIterations = Number(lineage && lineage.iterations);
  const iterations = Number.isFinite(rawIterations) && rawIterations > 0 ? Math.floor(rawIterations) : 12;
  const normalizedRetries = Number.isFinite(Number(maxRetries)) && Number(maxRetries) >= 0
    ? Math.floor(Number(maxRetries))
    : 0;
  const totalAttempts = normalizedRetries + 1;
  // base_cost_units is the GUARANTEED spend: one attempt, no retries. Retries only
  // fire on failure, so they are contingency, not a certainty -- the pre-dispatch
  // cap gates on base_cost_units below. estimated_cost_units keeps the old
  // worst-case-if-every-attempt-fails total for logging/visibility only; treating
  // it as guaranteed spend used to block legitimate long configs (e.g. a
  // 20-iteration lineage with the default 5 retries) before a single attempt ran.
  const baseCostUnits = iterations * guards.cost_units_per_iteration;
  return {
    ...guards,
    iterations,
    total_attempts: totalAttempts,
    base_cost_units: baseCostUnits,
    estimated_cost_units: baseCostUnits * totalAttempts,
  };
}

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && rg -n -A20 -B5 "expansive|frontier" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/convergence/convergence.md | head -80
63-| `sliding-window` | Uses a bounded graph-novelty window, defaulting to 5 snapshots, while preserving the ordinary legal-stop and synthesis path. |
64-| `divergent` | Computes convergence normally, but translates an eligible legal STOP into a three-seat direction-selection pivot and continues from the selected focus. |
65-
66-#### Expansive Depth Mode
67-
68:`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a frontier of follow-up questions beside the questions seeded at init. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, `budgetSplit` 0.5/0.35/0.15 for depths 1, 2 and 3, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to `maxIterations`. An absent flag means `default`, which renders the same bytes as before; `research-depth-mode-golden.vitest` proves it.
69-
70:After the `minIterations` floor, an expansive run with open or active frontier nodes turns a soft stop candidate into CONTINUE with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. The veto never creates a stop of its own and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Under `--convergence-mode=divergent` the pivot becomes eligible only once no frontier node is open or active.
71-
72-Tested in `stop-decision.vitest`.
73-
74-#### Divergent Pivot Contract
75-
76-Research pivots are eligible only when the workflow decision is `STOP` and the exact internal reason is `composite_converged` or `all_questions_answered`. The legal-stop and quality gates run before this branch. `maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `stuckRecovery`, `minIterationsNotReached`, `error`, `manualStop`, and `userPaused` are explicitly excluded. Max iterations, pause or cancellation, manual stop, and unrecoverable error therefore never dispatch a pivot Council.
77-
78-Each eligible pivot runs one native, in-process Council round with exactly three distinct seats: analytical, critical, and pragmatic.
79-If that round misses quorum, because a seat fails to return or returns a report that does not parse, the pivot reopens once as attempt 2 with three fresh seat outputs. A second quorum miss, or three valid seats that do not agree, fails the pivot closed. `maxCouncilSeatOutputs` caps one council attempt at 3 seat outputs, so a pivot records at most 6.
80-
81-- It does not use an external CLI.
82-- The transaction requires all three seat returns to be parse-valid and at least two seats to materially endorse the same candidate without a high-severity blocker.
83-- Research candidates come from persisted adjacent questions, contradiction or verification gaps, missing source classes, alternate evidence methods, and independent checks of recent findings.
84-- A completed pivot restores the selected candidate's focus and returns to the research loop; a failed pivot fails closed to synthesis.
85-
86-Every pivot dispatches three seats, or six when its first attempt misses quorum, so a run that pivots has proportionally higher model cost and duration than the same run under `default`, `off`, or `sliding-window`. Pivot evidence is isolated under `<artifactRoot>/divergent/pivots/<pivotId>/council/**`, including config, append-only state, three seat artifacts, deliberation, and report, plus an attempt-2 folder with its own seat artifacts and deliberation after a retry. This path is distinct from ordinary planning Council artifacts under a spec packet's `ai-council/**` tree.
87-
88----
89-
90-## 2. STOP CONTRACT
--
127-2. After 3 consecutive failed iterations, STOP with stop reason error.
128-3. If stuckCount >= stuckThreshold, enter stuck recovery.
129-4. If convergenceMode is off or stopPolicy is max-iterations, CONTINUE.
130-5. If all key questions have evidence-backed answers, nominate STOP. Otherwise compute composite convergence signals and nominate STOP when the weighted stop score > 0.60. With no candidate, CONTINUE.
131-6. If iteration count < minIterations, CONTINUE with minIterationsNotReached.
132:7. If depthMode is expansive and a frontier node is open or active, CONTINUE with frontierOpen.
133-8. Evaluate legal-stop gates for every STOP candidate except hard max-iteration stop.
134-9. Block every non-terminal STOP until a graph decision exists.
135-10. If gates pass, pivot for an eligible divergent reason or STOP normally; otherwise emit blockedStop and CONTINUE with recovery focus.
136-```
137-
138-### Composite Signal Weights
139-
140-| Signal | Weight | Min Iterations | Measures |
141-|--------|--------|----------------|----------|
142-| Rolling Average | `0.30` | 3 evidence iterations | Recent information yield |
143-| MAD Noise Floor | `0.35` | 4 evidence iterations | Signal vs noise in `newInfoRatio` |
144-| Question Entropy | `0.35` | 1 key question | Evidence-backed question coverage |
145-
146-The weighted stop score is normalized over active signals. STOP is nominated only when the normalized score is greater than `0.60`. See `convergence-signals.md` for exact signal rules and reporting.

| `fanout_executors` | N | repeatable `--executor=<type>` flags or `--executors=<json>`; each group accepts `--model`, `--config-dir`, `--reasoning-effort`, `--service-tier`, `--executor-timeout`, `--iters`, `--label`, `--count`; one expanded lineage selects `config.executor`, two or more select `config.fanout` | none (single-executor when absent) | N |
| `fanout_concurrency` | N | flag `--concurrency=N` | `2` | N |

**Fan-out default policy:** 0–1 `--executor` flags and no `--executors`, or an `--executors` matrix that expands to exactly one lineage → `config.executor` (single-executor, default, unchanged). 2+ `--executor` flags, an `--executors` matrix that expands to two or more lineages, or any `--count > 1` → `config.fanout`. Review fan-out uses strongest-restriction: any lineage active P0 → merged FAIL. Native and CLI fan-out lineages run under `fanout-run.cjs` in the capped pool so every lineage shares retries, artifact checks, and stop-policy validation. The native adapter calls the `deep/review` command with the internal `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir` override; it must not dispatch the LEAF `@deep-review` agent or a pasted phase-running prompt as a full-loop sub-agent. When fan-out includes `cli-opencode`, that lineage is an explicit parallel-detached OpenCode session with its own lineage directory and session id; this is the documented exception to the cli-opencode self-invocation guard. Single-executor `cli-opencode` remains cross-runtime only.

**Lifecycle policy:** `lineage_mode=auto` preserves the existing classifier behavior, `lineage_mode=resume` continues a valid packet, and `lineage_mode=restart` archives the resolved `{artifact_dir}` before any config write, phase init, or fan-out spawn. In `:auto`, an explicit `--restart` or `--lineage-mode=restart` flag is operator authorization for this archive move, so do not ask for a second confirmation. Preserve rollback by moving the timestamped archive back to `review/` if needed. A restart request must never silently downgrade to resume; if the archive step cannot run, halt with `STATUS=FAIL`.

**Stop policy:** `stop_policy=convergence` preserves normal deep-review behavior: legal convergence may stop before `maxIterations`. `stop_policy=max-iterations` makes convergence signals telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling; before `maxIterations`, broaden the next angle instead of synthesizing. Terminal manual pause, user stop, unrecoverable error, and `maxIterationsReached` still stop.

93-
94:**Fan-out default policy:** 0–1 `--executor` flags and no `--executors`, or an `--executors` matrix that expands to exactly one lineage → `config.executor` (single-executor, default, unchanged). 2+ `--executor` flags, an `--executors` matrix that expands to two or more lineages, or any `--count > 1` → `config.fanout`. Review fan-out uses strongest-restriction: any lineage active P0 → merged FAIL. Native and CLI fan-out lineages run under `fanout-run.cjs` in the capped pool so every lineage shares retries, artifact checks, and stop-policy validation. The native adapter calls the `deep/review` command with the internal `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir` override; it must not dispatch the LEAF `@deep-review` agent or a pasted phase-running prompt as a full-loop sub-agent. When fan-out includes `cli-opencode`, that lineage is an explicit parallel-detached OpenCode session with its own lineage directory and session id; this is the documented exception to the cli-opencode self-invocation guard. Single-executor `cli-opencode` remains cross-runtime only.
95-
96-**Lifecycle policy:** `lineage_mode=auto` preserves the existing classifier behavior, `lineage_mode=resume` continues a valid packet, and `lineage_mode=restart` archives the resolved `{artifact_dir}` before any config write, phase init, or fan-out spawn. In `:auto`, an explicit `--restart` or `--lineage-mode=restart` flag is operator authorization for this archive move, so do not ask for a second confirmation. Preserve rollback by moving the timestamped archive back to `review/` if needed. A restart request must never silently downgrade to resume; if the archive step cannot run, halt with `STATUS=FAIL`.
97-
98-**Stop policy:** `stop_policy=convergence` preserves normal deep-review behavior: legal convergence may stop before `maxIterations`. `stop_policy=max-iterations` makes convergence signals telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling; before `maxIterations`, broaden the next angle instead of synthesizing. Terminal manual pause, user stop, unrecoverable error, and `maxIterationsReached` still stop.

      'max_token_budget_per_lineage',

    cost_units_per_iteration: readPositiveRawConfigNumber(rawConfig, [
      'costUnitsPerIteration',

      'estimatedTokensPerIteration',
      'estimated_tokens_per_iteration',
    ], 'costUnitsPerIteration', 1),

function computeLineageBudgetUpperBound(lineage, guardsInput = {}, maxRetries = 0) {
  const guards = normalizeLineageBudgetGuards(guardsInput);
  const rawIterations = Number(lineage && lineage.iterations);
  const iterations = Number.isFinite(rawIterations) && rawIterations > 0 ? Math.floor(rawIterations) : 12;
  const normalizedRetries = Number.isFinite(Number(maxRetries)) && Number(maxRetries) >= 0
    ? Math.floor(Number(maxRetries))
    : 0;
  const totalAttempts = normalizedRetries + 1;
  // base_cost_units is the GUARANTEED spend: one attempt, no retries. Retries only
  // fire on failure, so they are contingency, not a certainty -- the pre-dispatch
  // cap gates on base_cost_units below. estimated_cost_units keeps the old
  // worst-case-if-every-attempt-fails total for logging/visibility only; treating
  // it as guaranteed spend used to block legitimate long configs (e.g. a
  // 20-iteration lineage with the default 5 retries) before a single attempt ran.
  const baseCostUnits = iterations * guards.cost_units_per_iteration;
  return {
    ...guards,
    iterations,
    total_attempts: totalAttempts,
    base_cost_units: baseCostUnits,
    estimated_cost_units: baseCostUnits * totalAttempts,
  };
}

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && rg -n -A20 -B5 "expansive|frontier" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/convergence/convergence.md | head -80
63-| `sliding-window` | Uses a bounded graph-novelty window, defaulting to 5 snapshots, while preserving the ordinary legal-stop and synthesis path. |
64-| `divergent` | Computes convergence normally, but translates an eligible legal STOP into a three-seat direction-selection pivot and continues from the selected focus. |
65-
66-#### Expansive Depth Mode
67-
68:`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a frontier of follow-up questions beside the questions seeded at init. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, `budgetSplit` 0.5/0.35/0.15 for depths 1, 2 and 3, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to `maxIterations`. An absent flag means `default`, which renders the same bytes as before; `research-depth-mode-golden.vitest` proves it.
69-
70:After the `minIterations` floor, an expansive run with open or active frontier nodes turns a soft stop candidate into CONTINUE with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. The veto never creates a stop of its own and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Under `--convergence-mode=divergent` the pivot becomes eligible only once no frontier node is open or active.
71-
72-Tested in `stop-decision.vitest`.
73-
74-#### Divergent Pivot Contract
75-
76-Research pivots are eligible only when the workflow decision is `STOP` and the exact internal reason is `composite_converged` or `all_questions_answered`. The legal-stop and quality gates run before this branch. `maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `stuckRecovery`, `minIterationsNotReached`, `error`, `manualStop`, and `userPaused` are explicitly excluded. Max iterations, pause or cancellation, manual stop, and unrecoverable error therefore never dispatch a pivot Council.
77-
78-Each eligible pivot runs one native, in-process Council round with exactly three distinct seats: analytical, critical, and pragmatic.
79-If that round misses quorum, because a seat fails to return or returns a report that does not parse, the pivot reopens once as attempt 2 with three fresh seat outputs. A second quorum miss, or three valid seats that do not agree, fails the pivot closed. `maxCouncilSeatOutputs` caps one council attempt at 3 seat outputs, so a pivot records at most 6.
80-
81-- It does not use an external CLI.
82-- The transaction requires all three seat returns to be parse-valid and at least two seats to materially endorse the same candidate without a high-severity blocker.
83-- Research candidates come from persisted adjacent questions, contradiction or verification gaps, missing source classes, alternate evidence methods, and independent checks of recent findings.
84-- A completed pivot restores the selected candidate's focus and returns to the research loop; a failed pivot fails closed to synthesis.
85-
86-Every pivot dispatches three seats, or six when its first attempt misses quorum, so a run that pivots has proportionally higher model cost and duration than the same run under `default`, `off`, or `sliding-window`. Pivot evidence is isolated under `<artifactRoot>/divergent/pivots/<pivotId>/council/**`, including config, append-only state, three seat artifacts, deliberation, and report, plus an attempt-2 folder with its own seat artifacts and deliberation after a retry. This path is distinct from ordinary planning Council artifacts under a spec packet's `ai-council/**` tree.
87-
88----
89-
90-## 2. STOP CONTRACT
--
127-2. After 3 consecutive failed iterations, STOP with stop reason error.
128-3. If stuckCount >= stuckThreshold, enter stuck recovery.
129-4. If convergenceMode is off or stopPolicy is max-iterations, CONTINUE.
130-5. If all key questions have evidence-backed answers, nominate STOP. Otherwise compute composite convergence signals and nominate STOP when the weighted stop score > 0.60. With no candidate, CONTINUE.
131-6. If iteration count < minIterations, CONTINUE with minIterationsNotReached.
132:7. If depthMode is expansive and a frontier node is open or active, CONTINUE with frontierOpen.
133-8. Evaluate legal-stop gates for every STOP candidate except hard max-iteration stop.
134-9. Block every non-terminal STOP until a graph decision exists.
135-10. If gates pass, pivot for an eligible divergent reason or STOP normally; otherwise emit blockedStop and CONTINUE with recovery focus.
136-```
137-
138-### Composite Signal Weights
139-
140-| Signal | Weight | Min Iterations | Measures |
141-|--------|--------|----------------|----------|
142-| Rolling Average | `0.30` | 3 evidence iterations | Recent information yield |
143-| MAD Noise Floor | `0.35` | 4 evidence iterations | Signal vs noise in `newInfoRatio` |
144-| Question Entropy | `0.35` | 1 key question | Evidence-backed question coverage |
145-
146-The weighted stop score is normalized over active signals. STOP is nominated only when the normalized score is greater than `0.60`. See `convergence-signals.md` for exact signal rules and reporting.

151-
152-Every non-terminal STOP candidate must pass the gate bundle before the workflow exits.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '20,40p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md
## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Seeding And Admission

The expansive run seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1. Leaf `frontier_proposal` rows are admitted under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps. Merge commits are pruned as `merge-no-own-diff`, and index rebuilds are pruned as `generated-artifact`.

### Dimension Coverage

The active node's next uncovered dimension is dispatched. A node needs one iteration per dimension in its universe, the four canonical dimensions plus any custom name. Each depth band gets only its `budgetSplit` share of `--max-iterations` (0.5, 0.35 and 0.15), so fund the depth-1 band with at least `2 * ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`.

### Stop Behavior

A review stop candidate becomes another iteration while nodes stay open. The `frontierOpen` veto applies to the review stop candidates, including `all_dimensions_clean` and the composite stop, after the minIterations floor.

### Pivot

The divergent review pivot runs only once the frontier is terminal. It seeds from terminal unanswered nodes and routes accepted candidates through admission.

### Fan-Out

Fan-out lineages receive a share of the seeded nodes. `runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` returns the merged review frontier and runs the review fan-in guard over the recorded partition.

    28	`expansiveDepth` in `deep-research-config.json` sets `maxDepth` to 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` to 6, and a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths. `antiConvergence.depthMode` defaults to `default`. The convergence script adds `depthMode` to its stop-decision config only when the value is `expansive`.
    29	
    30	### Stop And Next Focus
    31	
    32	While a frontier node is open, a composite convergence stop becomes another iteration. Next Focus steers to the shallowest open node. The iteration cap, the anti-convergence floor, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto, so the frontier never extends a run past them.
    33	
    34	### Fan-Out
    35	
    36	Fan-out lineages each receive a share of the seeded nodes. `runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` passes `depthMode` to a lineage only when it is `expansive`. `runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers and runs a fan-in guard over the recorded partition.

    20	| State management | 8 features | `research/deep-research-*.json`, `research/findings-registry.json`, reducer-owned strategy and dashboard surfaces |
    21	| Convergence | 6 features | [`references/convergence/convergence.md`](../references/convergence/convergence.md), workflow legal-stop gates, graph convergence hooks, minimum-iteration floor, divergent pivots |
    22	| Research output | 2 features | the research synthesis under `research/`, iteration files, reducer-backed negative knowledge surfaces |
    23	
   465	#### Description
   466	
   467	Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-research seeds depth-one frontier nodes from the topic, admits proposals under caps, and keeps a convergence stop open while frontier nodes stay open.
   468	
   469	#### How It Works
   470	
   471	`default` is the value when the flag is absent and runs unchanged. `frontier-admit.cjs` admits proposals into the node store, and the iteration cap, the floor, stuck recovery, pause, convergence off and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.
   472	
   473	#### Source Files
   474	
   475	See [`convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md`](../feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '1182,1198p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs; echo ---; rg -n "all_questions_answered|frontierOpen|consecutiveFailureCount" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs | head -12
}

  // An expansive run that still holds open frontier nodes turns a stop
  // candidate into another iteration. It never creates a stop, and everything
  // above it (cap, failures, stuck recovery, off, max-iterations, the floor)
  // still wins. A review clean candidate is a hard stop, so keying the veto on
  // soft candidates alone would leave a review run no way to spend the
  // iteration its open nodes need.
  const depthFacts = loopType === 'review' ? review : research;
  const vetoable = loopType === 'review' || !candidate.hardStop;
  if (config.depthMode === 'expansive'
    && vetoable
    && Number.isFinite(depthFacts.frontierOpen)
    && depthFacts.frontierOpen > 0) {
    return emit('CONTINUE', null, 'frontierOpen', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

.skilled/commands/deep/research.md:104:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 frontier nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as new questions under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), steers Next Focus to the shallowest open node, and turns a convergence stop into another iteration while frontier nodes stay open. It never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Fan-out lineages each get a share of the seeded nodes. |
.skilled/commands/deep/review.md:97:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1 (record the pilot range SHA; merges are pruned as `merge-no-own-diff` and index rebuilds as `generated-artifact`), admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, holds open nodes as `searchDebt`, dispatches the active node's next uncovered dimension, and turns a review stop candidate into another iteration while nodes stay open. A node needs one iteration per dimension in its universe, the four canonical dimensions plus any custom name, and the depth-1 band gets only `budgetSplit.depth1` (0.5) of `--max-iterations`, so set `--max-iterations` to at least `2 * ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions` to fund the depth-1 band. It never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Fan-out lineages each get a share of the seeded nodes. |

32:While a frontier node is open, a composite convergence stop becomes another iteration. Next Focus steers to the shallowest open node. The iteration cap, the anti-convergence floor, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto, so the frontier never extends a run past them.
32:While a frontier node is open, a composite convergence stop becomes another iteration. Next Focus steers to the shallowest open node. The iteration cap, the anti-convergence floor, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto, so the frontier never extends a run past them.
